======================================================================
COMPUTER NETWORKS KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Networking Fundamentals
Concept: Computer Network
Definition: A computer network is a collection of interconnected devices that exchange data.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in computer networking.
- Frequently covered in networking courses and interviews.
- Used in enterprise and Internet infrastructure.
Example:
Computer Network is widely used in modern computer networks.
Related Concepts:
OSI Model, TCP/IP, Routing, Switching, Security, Internet Protocols

Example:

Concept: MAC Address
Definition: A unique hardware identifier assigned to a network interface.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in computer networking.
- Frequently covered in networking courses and interviews.
- Used in enterprise and Internet infrastructure.
Example:
00:1A:2B:3C:4D:5E
Related Concepts:
OSI Model, TCP/IP, Routing, Switching, Security, Internet Protocols
